## 4. Detailed API Reference & Runnable Verification Snippets

### `blackboardNew` (`std/ai/btree_ai.aipl`)

- **Signature**: `pub fn blackboardNew(): Blackboard:`
- **Import**: `import * from "std/ai/btree_ai"` (or barrel `import * from "std/ai/ai_mod"`)
- **Receiver**: `None (free function)` | **Parameters**: `none` | **Returns**: `Blackboard`
- **Unit Test**: `tests/functions/ai/btree_ai_test.aipl` | **Example**: `examples/ex_238_ai_fsm_hierarchical_guard_agent_window.aipl`
- **Behavioral Summary**: Tests Behavior Tree Sequence, Selector, Parallel, Inverter, Cooldown, and Blackboard memory evaluation in `blackboardNew`. Concrete nominal inputs: zero arguments (`()`); exercises core body path returning/computing `Blackboard(entries = es)`.

```aipl
module tests.functions.ai.btree_ai_z1_test
import * from "std/ai/btree_ai"
import { Option, Result, Some, None, Ok, Err, assert, assertEqInt, assertEqStr, assertApproxEq } from "std/prelude"
pub fn test_btree_ai_blackboardNew_1(): int:
  let resNom = blackboardNew()
  let resEdge = blackboardNew()
  assertEqInt(resNom.entries.len(), 0, "blackboardNew nominal resNom.entries.len() == 0")
  assertEqInt(resEdge.entries.len(), 0, "blackboardNew boundary resEdge.entries.len() == 0")
  ret 0
```

---

### `blackboardSet` (`std/ai/btree_ai.aipl`)

- **Signature**: `pub fn blackboardSet(mut bb: Blackboard, key: str, val: int): void:`
- **Import**: `import * from "std/ai/btree_ai"` (or barrel `import * from "std/ai/ai_mod"`)
- **Receiver**: `None (free function)` | **Parameters**: `mut bb: Blackboard, key: str, val: int` | **Returns**: `void`
- **Unit Test**: `tests/functions/ai/btree_ai_test.aipl` | **Example**: `examples/ex_239_ai_btree_blackboard_visualizer_window.aipl`
- **Behavioral Summary**: Tests Behavior Tree Sequence, Selector, Parallel, Inverter, Cooldown, and Blackboard memory evaluation in `blackboardSet`. Concrete nominal inputs: `bb: Blackboard = Blackboard(entries = [BlackboardEntry(key = 0, val = 0)])`, `key: str = "aipl_test"`, `val: int = 1`; exercises core body path returning/computing `void state update`.

```aipl
module tests.functions.ai.btree_ai_z2_test
import * from "std/ai/btree_ai"
import { Option, Result, Some, None, Ok, Err, assert, assertEqInt, assertEqStr, assertApproxEq } from "std/prelude"
pub fn test_btree_ai_blackboardSet_2(): int:
  let arg_bb = Blackboard(entries = [])
  let edge_bb = Blackboard(entries = [])
  let arg_key = "test"
  let edge_key = "a"
  let arg_val = 2
  let edge_val = 0
  blackboardSet(arg_bb, arg_key, arg_val)
  blackboardSet(edge_bb, edge_key, edge_val)
  assertEqInt(arg_bb.entries.len(), 1, "blackboardSet nominal arg_bb.entries.len() == 1")
  assertEqInt(edge_bb.entries.len(), 1, "blackboardSet boundary edge_bb.entries.len() == 1")
  ret 0
```

- **Import**: `import * from "std/ai/btree_ai"` (or barrel `import * from "std/ai/ai_mod"`)

- **Unit Test**: `tests/functions/ai/btree_ai_test.aipl` | **Example**: `examples/ex_240_ai_goap_planner_and_utility_ai_window.aipl`
- **Behavioral Summary**: Tests Behavior Tree Sequence, Selector, Parallel, Inverter, Cooldown, and Blackboard memory evaluation in `blackboardGet`. Concrete nominal inputs: `bb: Blackboard = Blackboard(entries = [BlackboardEntry(key = 0, val = 0)])`, `key: str = "aipl_test"`; exercises core body path returning/computing `None`.

```aipl
module tests.functions.ai.btree_ai_z3_test
import * from "std/ai/btree_ai"
import { Option, Result, Some, None, Ok, Err, assert, assertEqInt, assertEqStr, assertApproxEq } from "std/prelude"
pub fn test_btree_ai_blackboardGet_3(): int:
  let arg_bb = Blackboard(entries = [])
  let edge_bb = Blackboard(entries = [])
  let arg_key = "test"
  let edge_key = "a"
  let resNom = blackboardGet(arg_bb, arg_key)
  let resEdge = blackboardGet(edge_bb, edge_key)
  assert(resNom.isSome() == false, "blackboardGet nominal resNom.isSome() == false")
  assert(resEdge.isSome() == false, "blackboardGet boundary resEdge.isSome() == false")
  ret 0
```

```aipl
  let arg_allNodes = [BehaviorTreeNode(id = 1, nodeType = BTreeNodeType.Sequence, children = [], cooldownSeconds = 2.0, actionName = "test")]

  assert(resEdge == BTreeNodeStatus.Success, "btreeTickNode boundary resEdge == BTreeNodeStatus.Success")
  ret 0
```
